We study black-box, sampling-based training-data leakage through a probabilistic lens, treating N samples from p(.|x) as an estimate of the output distribution and casting leakage signals as functionals of it. We extend the blind-baseline critique into the sampling regime: on WikiMIA a blind bag-of-words classifier reaches AUC 0.97 (TPR 0.90 at 5% FPR) and sampling adds nothing, while on an IID Pile split (MIMIR) neither self-concentration nor gold-continuation recovery significantly beats a blind baseline (incremental AUC 95% CI includes zero). Aggregate metrics hide the real harm. The same sampling verbatim-extracts training data for a tail of documents no blind attack can reach. On Pythia-6.9B, 83 of 500 Pile documents bearing a real identifier (16.6%; 21.3% of those bearing an email address) have that exact identifier reproduced AND not reproduced under a mismatched-prefix control, so each leak is attributable to that document, not to a globally common string. This per-document disclosure is invisible to aggregate AUC and grows with capacity (5.6% to 16.6% from 410M to 6.9B). The risk is uneven: identifier leakage is ~3x stronger in code than prose, though prose stays clearly positive and also grows with capacity (4.0% to 12.1%), while recovery of arbitrary held-out continuations is confined to code (+0.44 member gap on GitHub vs at most +0.014 on prose). Temperature and nucleus sampling matter little, a 16-token prefix suffices, and we detect no reduction from corpus deduplication. Privacy audits should report per-document extraction, decomposed by domain, not a single AUC. We release leakit, a black-box extraction-audit tool.

</details>


### [61] [Response Magnitude as a Dominant Signal for Held-Out CRISPRi Perturbation Effect Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00152)
*Mehrdad Shoeibi,Niloofar Yousefi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了CRISPRi扰动在单细胞转录组水平上的预测问题，发现一个简单的由四个标量组成的低维信号就能高度预测目标基因的扰动效应幅度，其表现超过深度神经网络编码器，且该幅度信号可跨细胞类型迁移，而表达信号迁移效果不佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在单细胞生物学中，预测CRISPRi扰动对保留目标基因的转录组效应幅度是一个重要的开放问题。已有研究表明简单基线方法往往能达到或超过深度扰动预测器，作者希望在VCC基准的严格目标基因划分下系统研究这一现象，找出驱动差距的具体低维信号并刻画其跨细胞类型的迁移特性。

Method: 作者在Virtual Cell Challenge (VCC)基准上采用严格的保留目标基因划分进行评估。目标变量为非靶向对照的log Anderson-Darling距离，研究发现该变量可由2,000维输入的四个确定性标量函数高度预测。作者比较了深度MLP编码器、四个标量上的线性回归、以及结合输入与四个标量的随机森林等多种模型，并通过预指定的对照实验将幅度增益归因于逐行对齐而非维度增加。此外，在两个外部CRISPRi筛选上评估了零样本迁移性能。

Result: 四个幅度标量上的线性回归超过了最强的x-only经典模型；随机森林在输入加四个标量上大幅优于深度证明型编码器。深度编码器倾向于塌缩到边际训练均值，标准补救措施无法弥合差距。在零样本迁移中，幅度型预测器转移为正收益，表达型预测器为负或无法解析。向深度编码器暴露幅度信号可改善迁移，但仍不如四标量线性回归。此外发现Anderson-Darling列衡量的是转录组范围内的响应广度而非目标基因效应强度。

Conclusion: CRISPRi扰动效应的可预测性主要来自一个低维幅度信号，而非复杂的高维多基因表达模式。简单线性模型在该任务上优于深度模型，且幅度信号具有跨细胞类型的正迁移能力。同时，用于迁移评估的Anderson-Darling指标衡量的是响应广度而非目标基因效应强度，这提示需要谨慎解释迁移评估结果。

Abstract: Predicting the magnitude of a CRISPRi perturbation's transcriptomic effect on held-out target genes is an important open problem in single-cell biology. Recent work has documented that simple baselines often match or exceed deep perturbation predictors on related protocols. We study this phenomenon on the Virtual Cell Challenge (VCC) benchmark under a strict held-out target-gene split, identify the specific low-dimensional signal that drives the gap, and characterize how it transfers across cell types. The target is the log Anderson-Darling distance from non-targeting controls, which is strongly predictable from four deterministic scalar functions of the 2,000-dimensional input. A deep MLP encoder with direct access to the full input collapses toward the marginal training mean, and standard remedies do not close the gap. A linear regression on the four magnitude scalars alone exceeds the strongest x-only classical model, while a Random Forest on the input plus the four scalars substantially outperforms our deep proof-of-concept encoder. Two pre-specified controls attribute the magnitude gain to per-row alignment rather than added dimensionality. Under zero-shot transfer to two external CRISPRi screens evaluated against a target-gene endpoint rebuilt from single-cell data, magnitude-only predictors transfer positively whereas expression-only predictors are negative or unresolved. Exposing magnitude to the deep encoder improves transfer over its expression-only counterpart, yet the encoder does not outperform a four-scalar linear regression on the same features. We also find that the Anderson-Darling column distributed with these screens measures transcriptome-wide response breadth rather than target-gene effect strength, so evaluating transfer against it scores a different outcome.

</details>


### [62] [Inference-Time Policy Alignment for Fair Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00175)
*Umer Siddique,Peilang Li,Conor Wallace,Yongcan Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种推理时公平性对齐框架，通过乘法策略塑形调整预训练深度强化学习策略的动作概率，在不更新基础策略参数的情况下实现基于福利的公平性目标，并保持核心任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度强化学习智能体通过优化标量奖励函数获得强性能，但部署后策略往往僵化且难以适应新的性能标准（如利益相关者的公平性偏好）。现有公平性强化学习通常假设公平性偏好先验已知，且需要基于公平性度量完全重训策略，成本高昂且不灵活。

Method: 受大语言模型推理时对齐启发，将推理时公平性对齐形式化为策略塑形问题，提出乘法策略塑形框架：通过依赖于动作的福利分数调整动作概率，从而在不修改基础策略参数的情况下实现公平性对齐。该框架具有通用性，兼容任意深度强化学习智能体。

Result: 在多个领域的广泛实验表明，推理时策略塑形能够显著改善基于福利的公平性目标，同时保持核心任务性能。

Conclusion: 推理时策略塑形为在不需要重新训练的情况下调整预训练强化学习策略的公平性提供了一种高效、通用的解决方案，避免了传统方法中代价高昂的完整重训过程。

Abstract: Deep reinforcement learning (RL) agents achieve strong performance by optimizing scalar reward functions. However, once deployed, the policies of these RL agents are often rigid and costly to adapt to new performance criteria. For instance, an agent trained to maximize expected cumulative reward may not accommodate previously unknown stakeholder preferences. Existing approaches to achieve fairness, a type of preference, in RL typically assume that such preferences are known a priori and require complete retraining of the policy under a fairness-oriented metric. Inspired by inference-time alignment in large language models, we investigate the problem of steering a pretrained RL policy toward welfare-based fairness objectives at inference time without updating the base policy's parameters. We formalize inference-time fairness alignment as a policy shaping problem and propose a multiplicative policy shaping framework that adjusts action probabilities using action-dependent welfare scores, thus requiring no modification to the base policy. Our framework is general and compatible with any deep RL agent. Through extensive experiments across multiple domains, we demonstrate that inference-time policy shaping substantially improves welfare-based fairness objectives while preserving core task performance.

</details>


### [63] [AutoCause: A Python framework that automates expert decisions in environmental time-series causal discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00198)
*Marco Ruiz,Miguel Arana-Catania,David R. Ardila,Rodrigo Ventura*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AutoCause 是一个开源 Python 工作流，用于环境时间序列因果发现，它记录每个分析决策、从扩展的因果审计模块中派生默认值并允许领域知识覆盖，以解决专家选择不一致导致图谱不可比较、不可复现和不可审计的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 环境时间序列因果发现需要专家就方法选择、条件独立检验、滞后范围、样本量充分性、多重检验控制和证据解读做出决策，但不同数据集上应用不一致会产生无法比较、复制或审计的因果图。

Method: AutoCause 记录每个决策，从扩展的因果审计模块中派生默认值，并允许领域知情覆盖。工作流封装了来自三个家族的四种既定因果发现方法，添加非因果参考模型，并根据方法计数支持对链接进行分级。在 145 个来自 DGP-Atlas、TimeGraph 和基于拓扑的 CausalRivers 参考数据集上进行了评估。

Result: 各方法在参考图上恢复了互补部分。在合成基准上，多数支持的链接比单一方法的链接更精确，但针对河流拓扑则不然。AutoCause 将不一致的专家实践转化为可审计、可重复的分析。

Conclusion: AutoCause 成功将不一致的专家实践转化为可审计、可重复的分析流程，但因果解读仍由分析师负责。

Abstract: Environmental time-series causal discovery requires expert decisions about method choice, conditional-independence tests, lag horizons, sample-size adequacy, multiple-testing control, and evidence interpretation. Applied inconsistently across datasets, these choices yield graphs that cannot be compared, reproduced, or audited. We present AutoCause, an open-source Python workflow that records each decision, derives defaults from an extended causal-audit module, and admits domain-informed overrides. The workflow wraps four established causal-discovery methods from three families, adds non-causal reference models, and grades links by method-count support. On 145 datasets from DGP-Atlas, TimeGraph, and a topology-derived CausalRivers reference, the methods recover complementary parts of the reference graphs. Majority-supported links are more precise than single-method links on the synthetic benchmarks but not against river topology. AutoCause converts inconsistent expert practice into an auditable, repeatable analysis; causal interpretation remains with the analyst. Available at https://github.com/marcoruizrueda/autocause.

</details>


### [64] [A Physics-Chemistry-Informed Neural Network (PCINN) for Real-Time Spatial-ALD Coverage Prediction and Reliable Kinetics Inversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00212)
*Ning Hu,Chang Liu,Yunlei Jiang,Yuan Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出物理-化学信息神经网络（PCINN）作为空间原子层沉积（SALD）的混合代理模型，以实时速度（约7ms/次查询，比CFD快约5×10^4倍）实现CFD级别精度（R²_log=0.998），并且不是黑箱——小网络仅学习操作条件到近壁浓度的映射，表面动力学作为硬编码可训练化学层集成。作者还进行了完整的可辨识性分析（Fisher信息、轮廓似然），发现吸附能E_ads和解吸速率k_des可靠可辨识，而吸附速率k_ads在单温度下不可独立辨识；多温度下前因子ν与E_ads沿弱可辨识退化谷分布，其斜率可用于诊断未建模的位点异质性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SALD是工业原子层沉积（ALD）的主要大气压高通量途径，但其设计和控制受限于表面覆盖率预测成本：高保真CFD计算太慢无法用于操作窗口扫描，而解析模型又遗漏了如气帘等传输调制效应。需要一种兼具CFD精度和实时速度的代理模型，同时保持可解释性和可逆性。

Method: PCINN架构采用混合方法：一个小型神经网络仅学习操作条件到近壁浓度的闭合关系，而已知的表面动力学作为硬编码的、可训练的化学层沿基底轨迹积分。这种单标量瓶颈设计使得模型在稀疏数据下仍保持准确，且可解释、可逆。作者进一步实施完整的可辨识性分析，包括Fisher信息矩阵和轮廓似然方法，并推导了前因子ν与吸附能E_ads之间退化关系的解析形式。

Result: 查询时间约7毫秒，比CFD求解快约5×10^4倍；测试R²_log=0.998，留一法R²_raw=0.974，仅需30个训练案例，覆盖覆盖率四个数量级。可辨识性分析表明：E_ads和k_des稳健可辨识，k_ads在单温度下仅能辨识k_ads*c_wall乘积形式；四温度下ν与E_ads沿斜率0.065 eV/decade的退化谷绑定（解析推导为k_B·T_eff·ln(10)）。七化学失配矩阵显示该斜率对任何单一Arrhenius失配不变，仅当出现第二个热激活过程时才偏移，可作为未建模位点异质性的诊断指标。

Conclusion: PCINN为SALD过程提供了一种兼具CFD精度、实时速度、可解释性和可逆性的混合代理模型，同时通过严格的可辨识性分析界定了参数识别的边界。该研究的退化解斜率为一个实用的可靠性诊断工具——斜率偏离解析预测值即标志存在未建模的位点异质性。值得注意的是，数据来自已知真值的模拟并由相同动力学形式反演，因此本研究验证的是管线的自洽性和可辨识性边界，而非真实物理参数。

Abstract: Spatial atomic layer deposition (SALD) is a leading atmospheric-pressure, high-throughput route to industrial ALD, but design and control are limited by the cost of predicting surface coverage: high-fidelity CFD is far too slow for operating-window scans, while analytic models miss transport modulation such as the gas curtain. We present a physics-chemistry-informed neural network (PCINN), a hybrid surrogate with CFD-level accuracy at real-time speed: a query returns coverage in about 7 ms, roughly 5x10^4 times faster than a CFD solve, reaching a test R^2_log = 0.998 (leave-one-out R^2_raw = 0.974) from only 30 training cases spanning four orders of magnitude in coverage.
  The architecture is not a black box: a small network learns only the operating-condition to near-wall concentration closure, while the known surface kinetics is a hard-coded, trainable chemistry layer integrated along the substrate trajectory. This single-scalar bottleneck keeps it accurate under sparse data, interpretable and invertible.
  We add a full identifiability analysis (Fisher information, profile likelihood). The adsorption energy E_ads and desorption rate k_des are robustly identifiable; k_ads is not separately identifiable at a single temperature (only k_ads*c_wall is). Across four temperatures the prefactor nu and E_ads bind along a weakly identifiable degeneracy valley of slope 0.065 eV/decade, derived analytically as k_B T_eff ln(10) and turned into a reliability diagnostic: a seven-chemistry mismatch matrix shows it is invariant under any single-Arrhenius mismatch and shifts only when a second thermally activated process appears, so a slope departure flags unmodelled site heterogeneity.
  Data come from simulation with known ground truth inverted by the same kinetic form, so the study verifies pipeline self-consistency and the identifiability boundary, not real parameters.

</details>


### [65] [Verifier-Induced Support Reshaping in On-Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00220)
*Shaohang Wei,Zikun Su,Feifan Song,Wen Luo,Wei Li,Guangyue Peng,Houfeng Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了带可验证奖励的在线强化学习（RLVR）会重塑策略的有效支持集——虽然能提升当前目标的性能，但会让后续目标的成功轨迹变得难以采样和强化，导致"验证器诱导的支持重塑"现象。作者在数学推理和受约束指令跟随两个任务、两个模型族上系统验证了该现象，并指出端点改进并不能保证未来的可训练性或联合能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR在很多任务上取得了显著成功，但其对策略"支持集"（即可采样到的成功轨迹分布）的长期影响尚未被系统研究。理解RLVR是否会在提升当前目标的同时，损害策略在其他目标上的可采样性，对于评估其可训练性和持续学习能力至关重要。

Method: 作者提出"有效可奖励支持"（effective rewardable support）概念，定义为在固定采样预算内可到达的成功轨迹集合。通过重复验证器打分采样、双向训练（Math-RLVR与IF-RLVR交替/顺序训练对方验证器）、token分布分析、受控的开头干预实验，以及测试参考策略约束、路由先验和在线蒸馏等缓解方案，系统研究RLVR对支持集重塑的影响。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B-Base的IFEval上，Math-RLVR使pass@1提升6.5个百分点，但best@32下降9.8个百分点，两个模型和两个IF基准上均出现该分歧。IF-RLVR使数学回复从逐步推导转向直接答案，降低了各采样预算下的best@k和后续Math-RLVR的奖励变化度。变化集中在开头几个token，RLVR主要重排基座策略已有的开头，而选定的开头因果地影响数学可搜索性。现有缓解方案仅部分保留跨任务支持。

Conclusion: 在线RLVR的端点改进不应被等同于未来可训练性或联合能力的保证——验证器诱导的支持重塑是一个普遍且值得警惕的副作用。作者强调需要在RLVR训练中显式监控和保持支持集的覆盖范围，而不是仅关注当前目标的指标改进。

Abstract: We show that on-policy reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) can improve the current objective while making successful behaviors for later objectives too rare to sample and reinforce. We call this verifier-induced support reshaping and define effective rewardable support as successful trajectories reachable within a fixed rollout budget. Across two model families, we study this effect through repeated verifier-scored sampling and bidirectional training on mathematical reasoning and constrained instruction following, including sequential training with the opposite verifier. Math-RLVR raises average instruction-following success but reduces the number of prompts with any successful response under repeated sampling. On IFEval with Qwen3-8B-Base, pass@1 rises by 6.5 percentage points while best@32 falls by 9.8 percentage points, and the same divergence appears across both models and IF benchmarks. Conversely, IF-RLVR shifts math responses from step-by-step openings toward direct answers, lowers best@k across sampling budgets, and reduces reward variation for later Math-RLVR. Token-distribution analyses and controlled opening interventions show that these changes concentrate in the first few response tokens. RLVR mainly reranks openings already available in the base policy, and the selected opening causally affects math searchability. The tested reference-policy constraints, routing priors, and on-policy distillation preserve cross-task support only partially; MathIF and ReasonIF show that marginal gains translate only partly into responses that are both correct and constraint-following. Therefore, endpoint improvements do not guarantee future trainability or joint capability under on-policy optimization. Code is available at https://github.com/sylvain-wei/verifier-induced-support-reshaping

</details>


### [66] [Similarity-Aware Machine Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00246)
*Madhavan Citalamangalam Kumaran,Midhun Parakkal Unni,Vicky Kouni,Haripriya Harikumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种保留感知（retain-aware）的模型反学习定位方法，在选择要修改的模型参数时同时考虑被遗忘数据和保留数据的重要性，并引入保留相似评估集来直接衡量附带损害，在CIFAR-10和ResNet18上的11个实验设置中一致降低了附带损害同时改善了标准反学习指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于定位（localization）的反学习方法仅根据遗忘集（forget-set）的重要性选择参数，忽略了这些参数在保留数据集（retained dataset）中的作用，常常对语义相似的保留样本造成附带损害（collateral damage）。作者旨在解决这一局限。

Method: 1) 提出保留感知的定位方法：同时考虑参数对遗忘数据和保留数据两者的重要性来选择参数子集；2) 引入保留相似评估集（retain-similar evaluation set），基于模型嵌入空间中的余弦相似度构建，用于直接量化附带损害。

Result: 在CIFAR-10数据集和ResNet18模型上的11个实验设置中，该方法一致地降低了附带损害，同时改善了标准的反学习评估指标，验证了保留感知定位在相似感知反学习中的有效性。

Conclusion: 保留感知的定位方法能有效平衡遗忘效果与保留数据完整性，为机器反学习提供了一种更精细的参数选择策略，减少对语义相近样本的负面影响。

Abstract: Machine unlearning removes the influence of user-specified training examples from a trained model, avoiding the need to retrain it from scratch. Localization-based methods improve unlearning efficiency by identifying a subset of influential model parameters. However, existing approaches select parameters based solely on forget-set importance, neglecting their role in retained dataset and often causing collateral damage to semantically similar retained examples. We address this limitation with a retain-aware localization method that considers parameter importance to both forgotten and retained data. We also introduce a retain-similar evaluation set, constructed using cosine similarity in the model embedding space, to directly measure collateral damage. Across eleven experimental settings on CIFAR-10 dataset and ResNet18 model, our method consistently reduces collateral damage while improving standard unlearning metrics, demonstrating the effectiveness of retain-aware localization for similarity-aware machine unlearning.

</details>


### [67] [Stabilized Best-of-$K$ Training for Neural Combinatorial Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00296)
*Melveena Jolly,Midhun Xavier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对POMO训练中的Leader Reward方法进行了窄范围扩展测试：将其二元(leader/non-leader)区分替换为基于采样预算K的稳定秩(rank)信号。在固定POMO架构、3,050-epoch训练计划和TSP-100测试集下，Leader Reward重现在100起點、8增强贪心解码下获得7.7662，与论文报告值匹配。在独立采样下，稳定K=8方法在三个配对训练种子中均降低了Best-of-8成本(7.7944对7.8136)，但该观察仅为估计层面的、解码器特定的结果，不主张普遍优越性或SOTA地位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索对现有POMO训练框架中Leader Reward模块的轻量改进——用稳定的秩信号替代二元区分，以在有限改动下测试是否能在采样评估场景中带来更优的Best-of-K性能。

Method: 在POMO架构上，将Leader Reward的二元leader/non-leader区分替换为按采样预算K索引的稳定秩(rank)信号；固定3,050-epoch训练计划与TSP-100测试集，使用100-start、8-augmentation贪心解码进行重实现验证，并在独立采样和配对训练种子设置下比较Best-of-8成本。

Result: Leader Reward重实现获得7.7662(与报告的7.766一致)；稳定K=8方法在三个配对训练种子下Best-of-8成本均低于Leader Reward(7.7944对7.8136)。但Leader Reward在采样K=1时更优，且在其原始增强贪心协议下仍略优。

Conclusion: 稳定秩信号在特定解码器(Best-of-8独立采样)和受限种子数下显示一致性改进，但结论仅限估计层面、依赖具体解码器设置；因种子数(3)低于六种子测试下限，且Leader Reward在某些配置(K=1, 增强贪心)下仍更优，故不作无偏估计、普适优越性或SOTA声明。

Abstract: Leader Reward modifies POMO training to emphasize the best trajectory produced by repeated inference. We test a narrow extension: replace its binary leader/non-leader distinction with a stabilized rank signal indexed by a sampling budget $K$. With the POMO architecture, 3,050-epoch schedule, and TSP-100 test set held fixed, the Leader Reward reimplementation obtains $7.7662$ under 100-start, 8-augmentation greedy decoding, matching the reported $7.766$ at its displayed precision. Under independent sampling, the stabilized $K=8$ recipe lowers realized Best-of-8 cost in all three paired training seeds: $7.7944$ versus $7.8136$. This observation is estimation-only and decoder-specific: three seeds are below the six-seed testing floor, Leader Reward is better at sampled $K=1$, and it remains slightly better under its original augmented-greedy protocol. We make no unbiased-estimator, universal superiority, or state-of-the-art claim.

</details>


### [68] [Abstention as an Action Can Kill Both the Reward Gradient and the KL Anchor: Collapse Law and Repair for Error-Penalized Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00301)
*Xujun Che,Yuchen Yuan,Weida Zhao,Chenyang Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了错误惩罚评分规则（1/-λ/0）条件下，KL锚定梯度学习者的行为。理论证明，当弃权是离散动作时，该学习者的奖励梯度和锚定恢复力会被同一门饱和因子共同扼杀，导致模型在覆盖崩塌的同时漂移向全部弃权，且平均训练奖励像1/t一样上升至零，使曲线看似改善实为恶化。优势估计器还会将设计惩罚暗中替换为有效惩罚1，把决策阈值从Chow阈值移至1/2。修复方案是结构性改变：训练阶段强制输出置信度报告（使用严格适当评分加正确性奖励），部署时才通过阈值决定弃权。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 错误惩罚评分规则被视为对抗幻觉的标准做法，但作者发现该规则下的直接梯度学习可能产生与设计意图完全相反的行为——模型不是变得更严格地拒答错误问题，而是全面弃权，甚至包括原本能正确回答的问题。理解这一机制失效的根源并找到修复方案至关重要。

Method: 作者对KL锚定梯度学习者在带弃权动作的离散行动空间中的动态进行理论分析，推导奖励梯度与锚定恢复力之间的耦合关系；随后通过模拟实验和在两个规模的语言模型上进行消融实验验证理论预测，并测试提出的修复方案（训练期强制置信度报告+严格适当评分，部署期阈值化弃权）。

Result: 理论证明：在显式条件下（全答的期望得分低于零且提示读取受限），模型向全弃权漂移，训练奖励以1/t速率趋近零。优势估计器进一步恶化问题，将有效惩罚归一化为1，使阈值从λ/(1+λ)移向1/2。所提修复方案通过消除门饱和因子使校准最优成为吸引子。实验证实：规则使模型在十步内弃掉仍能正确回答的问题，消融实验隔离了因果，报告级训练同时提升覆盖率、准确率和校准度。

Conclusion: 直接对带离散弃权动作的错误惩罚评分规则做梯度学习是结构性地失败的：门饱和效应使奖励梯度和锚定力同归于尽。解决方案是解耦动作与学习目标——在训练阶段强制输出置信度报告并优化严格适当评分，把弃权决策推迟到部署阶段，通过阈值化报告实现。

Abstract: Error-penalized scoring rules ($+1$ for a correct answer, $-λ$ for a wrong one, $0$ for abstaining) are increasingly prescribed against hallucination: a rational agent facing such a rule answers exactly when its correctness probability exceeds Chow's threshold $t^\ast=λ/(1+λ)$. We prove that a KL-anchored gradient learner can do the opposite. When abstention is a discrete action, the reward gradient and the anchor's restoring force are throttled by the same gate-saturation factor and die together: under explicit conditions (among them, blanket answering loses score in expectation and prompts share a bounded readout) the model drifts toward refusing everything, its mean training reward rising to zero like $1/t$ in training time $t$, so the curve reads as improvement while coverage collapses. The advantage estimator compounds the failure: in its sparse-answer regime, group normalization silently replaces every designed penalty with an effective penalty of one, moving the learned threshold from $t^\ast$ to $1/2$. The repair is structural: train a mandatory confidence report with a strictly proper score plus a correctness reward, and abstain only at deployment by thresholding the report. The always-emitted report has no gate to saturate, so no shared factor can kill its reward gradient and its anchor together, and its calibrated optimum is attracting. Simulations confirm every prediction, and experiments on language models at two scales confirm the mechanism live: the rule silences questions the models demonstrably still solve within ten optimizer steps, an ablation isolates the cause, and report-level training raises coverage, accuracy, and calibration together.

</details>


### [69] [Agentic Bayesian Optimization through Surrogate-Augmented Autoresearch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00316)
*Paul Brunzema,Louis Tiao,Nhat Le,Kevin De Angeli,Yao Xuan,Djordje Gligorijevic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"智能体贝叶斯优化"（agentic Bayesian optimization）范式，让LLM智能体作为BO循环中的核心决策者，同时以贝叶斯后端提供不确定性感知的优化支撑。作者实现了Sara（代理增强型自动研究智能体）和lenz（模块化BoTorch后端），在合成与真实基准上证明该方法在保持标准BO可靠性的同时，利用自然语言先验超越现有方法，并能在动态场景中实时重构优化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯优化（BO）依赖通用统计先验实现样本高效优化，但更丰富的领域先验难以通过定制核函数或问题结构编码进BO。LLM可以将自然语言、代码和文档中的非正式先验直接提供给优化器，但现有LLM-BO方法要么将LLM固定在单一角色（代理模型、采集函数代理或配置接口），要么赋予其过度宽泛的控制权，牺牲了BO可靠性的系统性探索能力。因此需要一种既能利用LLM灵活性与知识，又能保持BO可靠性的新范式。

Method: 提出agentic Bayesian optimization范式：LLM智能体作为BO循环的中心决策者，负责配置问题、查询贝叶斯后端、选择并提交评估，可在运行中调整优化策略（如收紧边界、切换采集函数、提出定向评估或根据新指令重构问题）。贝叶斯后端提供不确定性感知的优化基础。具体实现包括Sara（代理增强型自动研究智能体）和lenz（基于BoTorch的模块化后端，智能体可通过结构化接口检查和修改）。

Result: 在合成和真实世界基准测试中：1) Sara在无先验知识情况下保持了最先进BO的可靠性；2) 优于基于LLM的基线方法；3) 利用自然语言先验超越了标准BO的性能。在动态设置中，Sara展示了实时重构完整优化问题的能力，这是标准BO此前不具备的功能。

Conclusion: 智能体贝叶斯优化成功弥合了LLM领域知识与BO系统可靠性之间的鸿沟，使非正式的自然语言先验可以无缝融入优化流程，同时通过贝叶斯后端保证了不确定性感知的系统性搜索。该范式在动态优化场景中展现出独特优势，为未来LLM与BO深度融合提供了新方向。

Abstract: Bayesian optimization (BO) has become the standard tool for sample-efficient optimization and owes its efficiency to uncertainty-aware search driven by generic statistical priors. Richer domain priors can improve BO in principle, but encoding them through tailored kernels or problem structure is difficult and rarely done in practice. LLMs can help sidestep this difficulty by making informal priors from natural language, code, and documentation directly available to the optimizer. However, existing LLM-based BO methods either insert the LLM into a fixed role (surrogate, acquisition proxy, or configuration interface) or hand it broad control, sacrificing the systematic exploration that makes BO reliable. We introduce agentic Bayesian optimization: a paradigm in which an LLM agent is the central decision maker in the BO loop while a Bayesian backend provides the uncertainty-aware optimization substrate. The agent configures the problem, queries the backend, selects and commits evaluations, and can revise the optimization strategy during the run by tightening bounds, switching acquisition functions, proposing targeted evaluations, or even reframing the problem following new instructions or observed evidence. We instantiate this idea in Sara, a surrogate-augmented autoresearch agent, and lenz, a modular BoTorch-based backend that the agent can inspect and modify through a structured interface. Across synthetic and real-world benchmarks, Sara preserves the reliability of state-of-the-art BO without prior knowledge, outperforms LLM-based baselines, and uses natural-language priors to improve beyond standard BO. We further demonstrate the practical value of agentic BO in dynamic settings, where Sara reconfigures the full optimization problem on the fly as requirements change, a capability not previously available in standard BO.

</details>


### [70] [Neural operator learning for collision-aware trajectory planning of spacecraft swarms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00320)
*Sidhdharth D. Sikka,Suyi Gao,Zehui Lu,Rongjie Lai,Shaoshuai Mou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种置换等变神经算子，可在单次前向传播中为整个航天器集群生成碰撞感知轨迹，并结合批量高斯-牛顿精化以确保精确轨道动力学。该方法无需最优轨迹标签即可自监督训练，从10颗航天器零样本泛化至1000颗，在超过11000个编目物体的拥挤轨道中匹配单智能体最优控制精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典轨迹优化方法在航天器集群规模增大时，由于成对安全约束数量随规模呈指数级增长而难以扩展；而学习型规划器通常无法跨集群大小或碎片密度泛化。亟需一种既能处理大规模集群又能应对拥挤轨道环境的快速可扩展轨迹规划方案。

Method: 提出置换等变神经算子，将航天器、目标与碎片的分布映射为整个集群的碰撞感知轨迹（单次前向传播）；配合批量高斯-牛顿精化步骤强制满足精确轨道动力学。训练采用自监督物理目标函数（无最优轨迹标签），并引入针对自身rollout生成的对抗威胁进行对抗训练。

Result: 在10颗航天器上训练后，零样本泛化到1000颗航天器及11000多个编目物体的场景，匹配逐智能体最优控制求解器的精度；能规避碎片盲基线无法规避的最坏情况威胁；集群内部邻近距离降低数倍。

Conclusion: 基于物理约束的算子学习为拥挤轨道中的大规模航天器集群提供了一种快速且可扩展的最优控制替代方案，兼顾了精确性与计算效率。

Abstract: Autonomous spacecraft swarms must plan fuel-efficient, collision-free maneuvers in increasingly congested orbits, yet classical trajectory optimization scales poorly as pairwise safety constraints multiply with swarm size, and learning-based planners rarely transfer across swarm sizes or debris densities. Here we introduce a permutation-equivariant neural operator that maps distributions of spacecraft, targets and debris to collision-aware trajectories for an entire swarm in a single forward pass, paired with a batched Gauss-Newton finish that enforces exact orbital dynamics. The operator is trained without optimal-trajectory labels, combining self-supervised physics objectives with adversarial threats generated against its own rollouts. Trained on ten spacecraft, it generalizes zero-shot to swarms of 1,000 amid more than 11,000 catalogued objects, matching a per-agent optimal-control solver's accuracy, evading worst-case threats that a debris-blind baseline cannot, and reducing proximity within the swarm several-fold. Physics-grounded operator learning thus offers a fast, scalable alternative to optimal control for crowded orbits.

</details>


### [71] [Ensemble of Unsupervised Deep Learning for Clustering Imbalanced Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00346)
*Pulock Das,Yina Hou,Md. Kamrozzaman Bhuiyan,Manar D. Samad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究深度聚类在类别不平衡表格数据上的表现，并提出了两种新的聚类集成方法（跨嵌入维度聚合、多数投票集成），在16个二分类表格数据集上验证了集成方法在ACC、NMI、ARI指标上优于单个聚类方法，表明深度聚类可作为不平衡场景下监督分类的强有力替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据不平衡是监督分类的主要挑战，多数类偏见会导致假阴性和分类准确率被高估。由于深度聚类在无标签条件下进行表示学习，理论上对类别不平衡具有免疫力，但此前缺乏对其在不平衡表格数据上性能的系统评估。

Method: 提出两种新颖的聚类集成方法：(1) 跨不同嵌入维度聚合深度聚类的分配结果；(2) 对表现最佳的聚类算法应用多数投票。在16个二分类表格数据集上引入不同程度的人工不平衡，全面评估多种SOTA深度聚类方法的性能。

Result: 实验表明不同深度聚类方法在不同不平衡水平下展现出各自的优势。平均而言，两种集成方法在ACC、NMI和ARI指标上均优于单个聚类方法，并对数据不平衡具有更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 在数据不平衡场景下，深度聚类可以作为监督分类的强有力替代方案，而所提出的集成方法进一步提升了聚类在不平衡数据上的效果与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Data imbalance poses a major challenge in supervised classification, where the majority-class bias contributes to false negatives and overestimates classification accuracy. Unsupervised deep clustering can be immune to class imbalance because representation learning for clustering is performed without class labels. Deep clustering has been proposed for images, languages, and graphs, while its application to tabular data has only emerged recently. This paper is among the first to examine the performance of state-of-the-art deep clustering methods under varying levels of data imbalance. We introduce two novel cluster ensemble approaches: one aggregates deep clustering assignments across different embedding dimensions, and the other applies majority voting to the best-performing clustering algorithms. Experiments on 16 binary tabular datasets with varying and artificially induced levels of imbalance reveal distinct strengths of different deep clustering methods. On average, our ensemble methods outperform individual clustering methods in ACC, NMI, and ARI scores, offering greater resilience to data imbalance when identifying ground-truth classes without supervision. Therefore, in an imbalanced data scenario, deep clustering can serve as a strong alternative to supervised classification.

</details>


### [72] [Modeling Unknown Nonlocal PDE Systems via Flow Map Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00400)
*Zhongshu Xu,Ying Li,Yanzhi Zhang,Dongbin Xiu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种流映射学习（FML）框架，用于直接从解数据中学习未知的非局部偏微分方程，通过学习有限时间演化算子而非底层非局部算子，实现对分数阶扩散和波动方程的准确长期预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非局部偏微分方程在众多应用中广泛出现，但由于非局部算子的存在，它们难以建模和学习。现有的方法通常需要逼近底层非局部算子，这在实际中面临困难。

Method: 提出流映射学习（FML）框架，该框架不学习或逼近底层非局部算子，而是直接学习模态空间或节点空间中的有限时间演化算子。针对谱表示和网格表示分别开发了两种互补的公式。

Result: 在一维和二维分数阶扩散方程及波动方程上的数值实验表明，该方法仅使用短观测窗口即可实现准确且稳定的长期预测。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法为学习未知非局部动力学提供了一个有效的数据驱动框架，无需显式评估非局部算子即可实现长期预测。

Abstract: Nonlocal partial differential equations arise in many applications but are often difficult to model and learn because of the presence of nonlocal operators. We present a flow-map learning (FML) framework for modeling unknown nonlocal PDEs directly from solution data. Rather than learning or approximating the underlying nonlocal operators, the proposed approach learns the finite-time evolution operator in either modal or nodal space. Two complementary formulations are developed for spectral and grid-based solution representations. Numerical experiments on one- and two-dimensional fractional diffusion and wave equations demonstrate accurate and stable long-time prediction using only short observation windows. The proposed approach provides an effective data-driven framework for learning unknown nonlocal dynamics without explicit evaluation of nonlocal operators.

</details>


### [73] [DSETA: A Dual-Stage Continual Learning Framework for Travel Time Prediction in Dynamic Traffic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00402)
*Yanming Lyu,Yue Cheng,Lingkun Li,Ruipeng Gao,Xinyue Liu,Hui Gao,Qiang Ni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DSETA是一个双阶段持续学习框架，用于乘车平台的ETA（预计到达时间）预测。它将学习过程分为日内（intra-day）和日间（inter-day）两个阶段，分别处理短期动态交通模式（如节假日、事故）和长期分布漂移（如季节性趋势、路网演变），并通过历史交通知识整合模块防止灾难性遗忘。在滴滴平台三个城市（北京、武汉、西安）的在线A/B测试中分别取得了6.62%、0.73%和2.40%的MAE降低，已部署至生产环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ETA预测方法无法同时适应短期不规则交通模式（如突发的拥堵事件）和长期分布漂移（如季节变化），且未有效区分长期趋势与短期波动，导致在真实场景中性能下降。因此需要一种能够持续学习并适应动态交通环境的ETA预测框架。

Method: 提出DSETA（Dual-Stage ETA）框架，包含两个学习阶段：(1) 日内学习阶段：完全依赖实时数据，动态适应由节假日或事故等事件引起的短期交通模式变化；(2) 日间学习阶段：利用短时间窗口的聚合历史数据，捕获长期分布漂移知识（如季节性趋势和交通网络演变）。此外设计了一个历史交通知识整合（Historical Traffic Knowledge Consolidation）模块，用于防止灾难性遗忘并保留常规交通模式的知识。

Result: 在滴滴真实数据集上的离线和在线实验中验证了DSETA的有效性和鲁棒性。在线A/B测试在北京、武汉、西安三个城市分别实现了MAE降低6.62%、0.73%和2.40%。该框架已成功部署于滴滴生产环境，每天处理数亿次请求。

Conclusion: DSETA通过双阶段持续学习机制有效解决了ETA预测中动态交通模式和分布漂移的适应问题，在工业大规模应用中展现了显著的性能优势和实践价值。

Abstract: Estimated Time of Arrival (ETA) prediction is a core component of intelligent transportation systems. As traffic congestion patterns become increasingly dynamic in large cities, maintaining high prediction accuracy poses a major challenge for ride-hailing platforms. Existing methods either fail to adapt to irregular traffic patterns and sudden congestion, or suffer from new distributions without disentangling long-term trends from short-term fluctuations, thereby degrading model performance in real-world scenarios. To address this challenge, we propose DSETA, an incrementally updated Dual-Stage ETA prediction framework. Specifically, the continual learning process is divided into \textit{inter-day} and \textit{intra-day} stages. We first design the \textit{intra-day} learning stage, which relies entirely on real-time data to enable dynamic adaptation to short-term traffic patterns caused by events like holidays or accidents. Next, we develop the \textit{inter-day} learning stage, which leverages aggregated historical data from a short time window to capture knowledge of long-term distribution shifts, such as seasonal trends and traffic network evolution. Subsequently, to prevent catastrophic forgetting and preserve knowledge of regular patterns, we explore a \textit{Historical Traffic Knowledge Consolidation} module. Finally, we validate DSETA's effectiveness and robustness through extensive offline and online experiments conducted on real-world datasets from DiDi's platform. Online A/B tests across three major cities including Beijing, Wuhan, and Xi'an consistently demonstrated performance gains, achieving MAE reductions of 6.62\%, 0.73\%, and 2.40\% respectively. This framework has been successfully deployed in DiDi's production environment, processing hundreds of millions of daily requests and validating its strong performance in industrial applications.

</details>


### [74] [Unleashing the Potential of Large Language Models: A Blueprint for Real-Time, Enterprise-Ready Deployments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00419)
*Muhammad Faizan Raza,Shuo,Yang,Satish Mahadevan Srinivasan,Joanna F. DeFranco*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种统一的、模式驱动的LLMOps架构，整合实时数据摄取、持续学习、RAG和人在回路反馈，通过四个映射到经典软件设计模式的组件（AIPO、STAR+FAR、SAGE、自动化反馈收敛阶段）来解决大语言模型在实时受监管场景中的知识过时、灾难性遗忘、幻觉和弱反馈回路问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模语言模型在实时、受监管的行业（如医疗和金融）中部署时面临四大核心挑战：知识过时（stale knowledge）、灾难性遗忘、幻觉以及缺乏有效的反馈回路。现有方案通常各自独立解决这些问题，缺乏统一的操作管道来平衡延迟、成本、准确性之间的权衡，同时满足审计和回滚等合规要求。

Method: 论文提出一个四组件统一的LLMOps架构，每个组件对应一个已建立的软件设计模式：1) AIPO（自适应摄取模式编排器），基于FreshStreamBench评估的实时数据摄入；2) STAR+FAR（稀疏时间适配器路由+新鲜度感知回放）用于持续学习；3) SAGE，一种SLO感知的自适应检索策略，逐查询预测段落预算以满足尾部延迟目标；4) 自动化反馈驱动的收敛阶段，包含RLHF触发机制。

Result: 该架构降低了延迟-成本-准确性之间的权衡（reduces latency-cost-accuracy trade-offs），同时为医疗和金融等高风险管理领域提供可审计性和回滚能力。

Conclusion: 通过模式驱动的模块化设计，该统一LLMOps管道能够在受监管的实时环境中可持续地保持模型知识的新鲜度与可靠性，在保证运营性能的前提下支持合规审计与安全回滚。

Abstract: Large language models deployed in real-time, regulated settings face knowledge staleness, catastrophic forgetting, hallucination, and weak feedback loops. We present a unified, pattern-driven LLMOps architecture integrating real-time data ingestion, continual learning, retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), and human-in-the-loop feedback into a single operational pipeline. Four contributions map to established software design patterns: an adaptive ingestion pattern orchestrator (AIPO) evaluated with FreshStreamBench; STAR+FAR continual learning with sparse temporal adapter routing and freshness-aware replay; SAGE, an SLO-aware adaptive retrieval policy predicting a per-query passage budget to meet tail-latency targets; and an automated feedback-driven convergence stage with RLHF triggers. The result reduces latency-cost-accuracy trade-offs while supporting auditability and rollback for high-risk sectors such as health care and finance.

</details>


### [75] [HP-JEPA: Hierarchical Partitioning for Multi-Resolution Graph Joint-Embedding Predictive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00491)
*Ruichen Xu,Jingxiang Qu,Wenhan Gao,Jiaxing Zhang,Linsey Pang,Ravid Shwartz-Ziv,Yann LeCun,Yuefan Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HP-JEPA，一种层级多分辨率图联合嵌入预测框架。它通过将图组织成由粗到细的有序分区分辨率库，在每个分辨率上分别进行上下文-目标潜在空间预测，并通过拼接或任务特定的分辨率加权来整合各分辨率的图表示，从而解决现有图JEPA方法因依赖单一固定图分区而导致表示偏向单一结构粒度的问题。在7个图分类和1个图回归基准上，HP-JEPA在8个任务中的6个上优于固定分辨率的Graph-JEPA基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图自监督学习中的联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）通常依赖单一预定义的图分区，这会使学习到的表示偏向某一种结构粒度，无法同时捕获不同图尺度上的互补模式，限制了表示的可迁移性和表达力。

Method: HP-JEPA将每个图组织为一个从粗粒度到细粒度的有序分区分辨率库，在每个分辨率上使用在线编码器、指数移动平均目标编码器和潜在预测器分别进行上下文-目标潜在空间预测。不同分辨率产生的图表示通过拼接或任务特定的分辨率加权进行整合，使下游模型能够融合局部、区域和全局结构信息。

Result: 在7个图分类基准和1个图回归基准上，HP-JEPA在8个任务中的6个上优于固定分辨率的Graph-JEPA基线。尺寸分层分析还显示，在三个代表性数据集的大多数图尺寸四分位数上，HP-JEPA的准确率均高于Graph-JEPA。

Conclusion: 层级多分辨率分区对于可迁移的图表示学习是有效的，HP-JEPA通过整合粗到细不同分辨率上的结构信息，能够获得比固定分辨率方法更优的图表示性能。

Abstract: Graph self-supervised learning aims to learn transferable representations from large-scale unlabeled graph data. Joint-embedding predictive architectures (JEPAs) avoid explicit negative-pair construction and raw-input reconstruction by predicting masked targets directly in latent space. However, existing graph JEPAs typically rely on a single predefined graph partition, biasing the learned representations toward one structural granularity and limiting their ability to capture complementary patterns at different graph scales. To address this limitation, we propose HP-JEPA, a hierarchical partitioning framework for multi-resolution graph joint-embedding prediction. HP-JEPA organizes each graph into an ordered bank of coarse-to-fine partition resolutions and performs context-target latent prediction separately at each resolution using an online encoder, an exponential-moving-average target encoder, and a latent predictor. The resulting resolution-specific graph representations are subsequently integrated through concatenation or task-specific resolution weighting, allowing downstream models to combine complementary local, regional, and global structural information. Experiments on seven graph classification benchmarks and one graph regression benchmark show that HP-JEPA outperforms the fixed-resolution Graph-JEPA baseline on 6 of 8 tasks, improving upon Graph-JEPA on most evaluated benchmarks. Size-stratified analyses further show that HP-JEPA achieves higher accuracy than Graph-JEPA in most evaluated graph-size quartiles on three representative datasets. These results highlight the effectiveness of hierarchical multi-resolution partitioning for transferable graph representation learning.

</details>


### [76] [Agentic Graph Token Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00542)
*Zhuoyi Peng,Yi Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种"智能体图标记推理"（agentic graph token reasoning）方法，将图标记化重新塑造为推理过程本身的一部分：模型在每一步自主选择要编码的图视图及其粒度，按需调用图编码器生成图标记并拼接到上下文中，实现逐步推理。通过三阶段训练流程（自监督阅读任务、标记鲁棒轨迹阶段和偏好优化），模型在七个图领域的评估中大幅超越基线，并能零样本迁移到未见领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于图标记的LLM图分析方法采用静态单次编码方式：在模型看到目标之前就预先编码一个固定的图视图（如节点、k跳邻域或聚类），且从不修订，这浪费了模型逐步推理的能力。作者希望将图标记的使用从静态的"一次性"模式转变为动态的、轨迹依赖的推理过程，使模型能够根据推理需求灵活选择图视图。

Method: 提出智能体图标记推理范式，核心是将图标记化融入推理循环：(i) 每步由模型自主选择要编码的图视图及粒度；(ii) 按需调用图编码器生成对应的图标记；(iii) 将生成的标记块拼接到运行上下文。训练采用三阶段流程：第一阶段用自监督任务让模型学会读取异构图标记；第二阶段采用标记鲁棒的轨迹训练并引入图标记一致性正则化器；第三阶段通过偏好优化奖励图标记证据与节点文本证据一致的轨迹。

Result: 在七个图领域的评估中，模型大幅超越广泛的基线方法，并且无需任何针对目标域的微调即可零样本迁移到未见领域。

Conclusion: 该工作将基于LLM的图分析从静态图标记编码器推向了一种图原生的智能体范式，展示了模型在推理过程中动态选择和获取图信息的能力，开辟了图标记推理的新方向。

Abstract: Graphs model relational data throughout science and industry, from citation networks to product co-purchase graphs. Because the nodes of many such graphs carry rich text, a growing line of work applies large language models (LLMs) to graph analysis. The most graph-native of these methods use graph tokens: a graph encoder compresses a graph view, such as a node, its k-hop neighbourhood, or a cluster, into a short block of continuous tokens that jointly encodes node attributes and topology and is read directly by the model. Existing methods, however, use graph tokens in a static single-shot manner: they encode one predefined graph view before the model has even seen the target and never revise it, leaving the model's step-by-step reasoning ability unused. We introduce agentic graph token reasoning, which recasts graph tokenization as part of the reasoning process itself. At each step, the model chooses which graph view to encode and at what granularity; a graph encoder is invoked on demand to materialise the corresponding graph tokens; and the resulting block is spliced into the running context. The model thus reasons step by step in the graph token space, and the tokens it reads are trajectory-dependent. We realise this with a three-stage training pipeline: (i) self-supervised tasks that teach the model to read heterogeneous graph tokens, (ii) a token-robust trajectory stage with a graph-token consistency regulariser, and (iii) preference optimisation that rewards trajectories in which the graph-token evidence and the node-text evidence agree. Across evaluations spanning seven graph domains, our models outperform a broad set of baselines by a large margin and transfer zero-shot to unseen domains without any per-target fine-tuning. More broadly, this work pushes LLM-based graph analysis from static graph-token encoders towards a graph-native agent paradigm.

</details>


### [77] [Crushing the Evidence: A Dual-Penalty Evasion Framework for Fooling White-Box Explainable AI Auditors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00566)
*Niraj Kumar,Harsh Kasyap*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种更具威胁的白盒梯度正则化逃逸攻击框架，通过连续嵌入双惩罚机制直接在模型参数中嵌入逃逸逻辑，无需OOD脚手架包装器，能在四个表格基准数据集上将目标特征归因压至接近零（<0.02），攻击成功率超过90%，从根本上绕过了条件异常检测防御。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的对抗性解释攻击依赖OOD检测器脚手架来切换预测，但其异常扰动足迹容易被现有防御机制识别。本文旨在揭示更隐蔽的攻击面——直接在模型参数中嵌入逃逸逻辑，生成平滑的分布内预测，不留任何异常足迹，从而绕过现有防御。

Method: 提出白盒、梯度正则化的逃逸攻击框架。采用连续嵌入双惩罚框架，在分布内数据训练过程中直接惩罚触发特征梯度，将逃逸逻辑原生嵌入模型参数，而非依赖外部的OOD脚手架包装器。

Result: 在四个基准表格数据集（COMPAS、German Credit、IEEE-CIS和Communities & Crime）上的实验表明，该方法能将目标特征归因压至接近零（<0.02），保持超过90%的攻击成功率，且能从根本上绕过条件异常检测。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了现有对抗性解释攻击防御的局限性——基于异常足迹检测的防御无法应对将逃逸逻辑嵌入模型参数的白盒攻击，需要新的防御思路来应对此类威胁。

Abstract: Post-hoc model explainers such as LIME, SHAP, and Integrated Gradients are widely deployed to audit models in high-stakes sensitive domains, including finance, healthcare, and social welfare. This ensures the model's transparency and acceptability. However, a few studies have examined potential attacks in the explainability pipeline. Adversaries can attempt to conceal algorithmic biases or backdoors using adversarial explanation attacks. These attacks have relied on scaffolding out-of-distribution (OOD) detectors that toggle predictions when queried by an explainer. Consequently, defenses have been developed to successfully neutralize these black-box attacks by identifying their anomalous perturbation footprints. In this paper, we demonstrate a critical vulnerability by introducing a more potent white-box, gradient-regularized evasion attack framework. By employing a continuous-embedding dual-penalty framework, we directly penalize trigger feature gradients during training on in-distribution data. Since our approach embeds the evasion logic natively into the model parameters, without relying on OOD scaffolding wrappers, it generates smooth, in-distribution predictions that leave no anomaly footprint. Empirical evaluations across four benchmark tabular datasets (COMPAS, German Credit, IEEE-CIS, and Communities & Crime) confirm that our method systematically crushes target feature attribution to near-zero (<0.02), maintains >90% Attack Success Rates, and fundamentally bypasses Conditional Anomaly Detection.

</details>


### [78] [Fairness Auditing: Lower Bounds on Company Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00568)
*Rachit Verma,Padala Manisha,Sujit Gujar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限预算下公平性审计的固有局限性。通过将公平性审计建模为公司与审计者之间的min-max博弈，推导出在两种审计机制（固定规模审计集和α容差审计）下，后审计群体均等偏差的下界，证明增加审计资源只能减少但无法消除公司规避审计的操纵空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作已证明黑盒公平性审计存在基本不可能性结果——表达能力足够强的模型可规避任何审计策略。本文旨在量化在有限审计资源下，审计后企业进行操纵的不可避免程度，以更好地理解公平性认证的根本限制。

Method: 将公平性审计形式化为一个min-max优化问题：计算能力无界公司与预算受限审计者之间的博弈。研究两种审计机制：(i) 有预算审计者使用固定规模审计集进行认证；(ii) 有预算的α容差审计者，额外要求审计集在α近似范围内估计认证模型的公平性。对两种设置均推导出最坏情况下后审计群体均等偏差的显式下界函数，并以线性分类器和神经网络分类器配合简单审计集构造启发式方法进行实证说明。

Result: 导出了两种审计机制下后审计群体均等偏差的显式下界，以审计预算、群体不平衡度和公平性容差为函数表达。实证结果验证了理论界限：增加审计资源可降低但无法消除后审计操纵空间，表明有限预算公平性认证存在根本性局限。

Conclusion: 有限审计预算下的公平性认证具有内在局限性：即使增加审计资源，企业仍保有可量化但不断缩小的操纵空间。这强调了在公平性审计实践中需要重新审视认证范围的边界，并在审计资源分配上做出更审慎的决策。

Abstract: Fairness audits are increasingly mandated in high-stakes applications such as hiring, lending, and automated decision-making. Recent work has established fundamental impossibility results for black-box fairness auditing, showing that sufficiently expressive models can evade any auditing strategy. We complement these results by quantifying the extent of unavoidable post-audit manipulation under finite audit resources. We formulate fairness auditing as a min-max optimization between a computationally unbounded company and a budget-constrained auditor. We study two auditing regimes: (i) a budgeted auditor that certifies fairness using a fixed-size audit set, and (ii) a budgeted α-tolerant auditor that additionally requires the audit set to estimate the fairness of the certified model within an α approximation. For both settings, we derive explicit lower bounds on the worst-case post-audit demographic parity deviation as functions of the audit budget, group imbalance, and fairness tolerance. Finally, we empirically illustrate these theoretical limits using simple audit-set construction heuristics with linear and neural network classifiers. Our results demonstrate that increasing audit resources reduces, but does not eliminate, the scope for post-audit manipulation, highlighting fundamental limitations of finite-budget fairness certification.

</details>


### [79] [CoSynFlow: Conformal Symplectic Neural Flows for Cross-System Prediction of Dissipative Hamiltonian Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00571)
*Baige Xu,Takaharu Yaguchi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CoSynFlow，一种保共形辛结构的神经流模型，用于学习耗散哈密顿动力学的连续时间解算子。通过组合辛剪切映射与显式共形缩放，CoSynFlow在构造上保持共形辛结构，并可泛化到未见系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经算子方法在求解微分方程时通常只优化预测精度，未显式编码动力学的几何结构。现有的结构保持模型（如SympNets和辛神经流）仅适用于保守哈密顿系统，而对于具有共形辛结构的耗散哈密顿系统，尚缺乏相应的结构保持方法。

Method: CoSynFlow将辛剪切映射与显式共形缩放相结合，从构造上保持共形辛结构。通过对有限维哈密顿描述符和耗散参数进行条件化，单个训练模型即可预测未见系统的解映射而无需重训练。

Result: CoSynFlow将结构误差保持在机器精度水平，在长时程预测中取得最低误差，并支持物理信息训练。

Conclusion: CoSynFlow成功将结构保持的神经算子方法从保守哈密顿系统推广到耗散哈密顿系统，在保持几何结构的同时实现了高精度泛化预测。

Abstract: Learning solution operators for differential equations is a central problem in scientific machine learning. However, many neural operator methods optimize prediction accuracy without explicitly enforcing the geometric structure of the dynamics. Structure-preserving models such as SympNets and Symplectic Neural Flows address this issue for conservative Hamiltonian systems by preserving the symplectic form. In dissipative Hamiltonian systems with conformal symplectic structure, however, the symplectic form evolves according to a conformal factor determined by the dissipation. We propose CoSynFlow, a conformal symplectic neural flow for learning continuous-time solution maps of dissipative Hamiltonian dynamics. CoSynFlow composes symplectic shear maps with explicit conformal scaling, preserving the conformal symplectic structure by construction. By conditioning it on a finite-dimensional Hamiltonian descriptor and the dissipation parameter, a single trained model predicts solution maps for unseen systems without retraining. CoSynFlow keeps the structure error at machine precision, attains the lowest long-horizon error, and admits physics-informed training.

</details>


### [80] [From field-scale to large-scale spectral libraries: Tabular foundation models in soil spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00608)
*Viacheslav Barkov,Jonas Schmidinger,Robin Gebbers,Martin Atzmueller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了TabPFN、CNN、Cubist、随机森林和PLSR五种回归模型结合不同降维策略（全光谱、PCA、PLS潜变量）在85个回归任务（涵盖田间尺度数字土壤制图和全球土壤光谱库）中的性能，发现TabPFN在全光谱上已超越所有经典基线，而PLS降维与TabPFN结合可获得最佳预测效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可见-近红外和中红外光谱虽能快速、低成本地预测土壤属性，但高维且高度共线性的光谱数据在机器学习建模中仍具挑战性，缺乏针对不同尺度和模型组合的系统性证据指导。

Method: 在来自公开土壤计量学基准数据集的85个回归任务上，系统比较了TabPFN、CNN、Cubist、随机森林和PLSR五种模型，并使用全光谱以及PCA和PLS降维特征进行建模。

Result: TabPFN在包括数万样本的大型光谱库任务中表现最佳；TabPFN直接使用全光谱即超越所有经典基线，表明显式降维并非强预测性能的必要条件；PLS对所有模型都是有效的降维策略，PLS潜变量与TabPFN结合取得最佳整体预测效果。

Conclusion: 研究表明PLSR的长期优势与现代表格基础模型TabPFN具有互补性，为跨运营尺度选择光谱校准模型提供了基于证据的指导。

Abstract: Visible and near-infrared (vis-NIR) and mid-infrared (MIR) spectroscopy enable rapid, cost-effective prediction of soil properties. Yet, translating high-dimensional, highly collinear spectra into accurate soil property predictions remains challenging, particularly when employing machine learning. We systematically investigated regression models and dimensionality reduction approaches for spectroscopic modeling across 85 regression tasks from open benchmark datasets in pedometrics spanning field-scale digital soil mapping and a global soil spectral library. We compared an in-context learning tabular foundation model (TabPFN), a convolutional neural network (CNN), rule-based regression (Cubist), Random Forest, and partial least squares regression (PLSR) using full spectra as well as features derived from principal component analysis (PCA) and partial least squares (PLS) latent variables. TabPFN consistently delivered the best overall performance across scales, including large spectral library tasks with tens of thousands of soil samples. Notably, TabPFN applied directly to full spectra already surpassed all classical baselines, showing that explicit dimensionality reduction is not strictly required for strong performance. Further improvements were achieved through PLS, which proved to be an effective dimensionality reduction strategy for all models. Combining PLS latent variables with TabPFN yielded the best predictions overall. Our findings provide evidence-based guidance for spectroscopic calibration model selection across operational scales, demonstrating that the long-standing advantages of PLSR and modern tabular foundation models complement each other in chemometrics.

</details>


### [81] [RHEA: Reliability-Harmonized Reconstruction and Assignment for Robust Multimodal-Attributed Graph Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00621)
*Yinlin Zhu,Di Wu,Ziyu Han,Zekai Chenm,Wang Luo,Miao Hu,Guocong Quan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Multimodal-attributed graphs (MAGs), whose nodes carry heterogeneous attributes such as text and images over a relational structure, have become a fundamental substrate for label-free entity grouping tasks, including community discovery and product segmentation. Existing MAG clustering methods effectively integrate complementary modalities when attributes are clean and complete, but degrade substantially under noisy or missing attributes because they implicitly assume equal modality reliability across all nodes. In practice, modality reliability is inherently node-specific: images may be corrupted or absent, while textual descriptions are incomplete or noisy. We argue that, under attribute homophily, graph neighborhoods naturally provide supervision-free evidence for estimating node-specific modality reliability. Based on this insight, we propose RHEA, a reliability-aware framework for MAG clustering that estimates node-specific modality reliability from neighborhood consensus and propagates this signal throughout the clustering pipeline. RHEA reconstructs unreliable or missing modalities from graph neighborhoods, adaptively weights modalities during reliability-aware fusion, and performs topology-aware optimal transport clustering with reliability-aware transport assignment and neighbor-consensus assignment distillation. Furthermore, the confidence of reconstructed representations is incorporated into the clustering objective, allowing uncertain reconstructions to contribute proportionally during optimization. Experiments on four MAG benchmarks under five attribute conditions show that RHEA consistently outperforms the strongest baseline, with NMI gains increasing as attribute quality deteriorates.

</details>


### [82] [Towards Effective Federated Multimodal Graph Learning via Navigating Multifaceted Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00623)
*Yinlin Zhu,Di Wu,Yi Zhang,Xunkai Li,Wang Luo,Wei-Jin Huang,Miao Hu,Guocong Quan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FedTCR，首个面向联邦多模态图学习（FMGL）的系统性算法。针对分散式多模态属性图（MAGs）中存在的任务、模态和拓扑三类异构性，采用两阶段训练范式（任务无关预训练+任务导向微调）和拓扑感知跨模态路由机制，通过结构信息增强的原型蒸馏与三级跨模态对比学习，在7个领域数据集上超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦多模态图学习（FMGL）将联邦图学习扩展到多模态属性图，但现有联邦图学习方法无法有效处理分散式MAGs中固有的多重异构性，包括跨客户端任务目标差异（任务异构）、模态质量和语义域不一致（模态异构）、以及低跨模态相关性的拓扑模式差异（拓扑异构）。

Method: FedTCR包含两个核心组件：(1) 两阶段训练范式：联邦任务无关预训练与隔离的任务导向微调相结合，以应对任务异构；(2) 拓扑感知跨模态路由机制：客户端通过图结构通知的拓扑感知重要性加权聚合，将模态特定知识蒸馏为紧凑原型；服务器评估跨客户端跨模态的原型关系，路由有信息量的原型作为对比参考，驱动三级跨模态对比学习，同时对齐跨客户端模态并保持判别性。

Result: 在7个领域的数据集上，FedTCR在图中心任务和模态中心任务上均优于现有最先进基线。

Conclusion: FedTCR是首个系统性解决FMGL中任务、模态和拓扑三重异构性的算法，通过两阶段训练和拓扑感知跨模态路由在设计上有效解耦了三类异构挑战，实验验证了其广泛领域的适用性和优越性。

Abstract: Multimodal-attributed graphs (MAGs), where nodes carry heterogeneous semantic content across multiple modalities while edges encode relational dependencies, have been widely adopted across diverse domains. Federated multimodal graph learning (FMGL) extends federated graph learning (FGL) to MAGs, enabling collaborative optimization across decentralized MAGs without exposing raw data. However, naively applying existing FGL methods to FMGL is insufficient, as they fail to navigate the multifaceted heterogeneity inherent in decentralized MAGs, including task heterogeneity across diverse client objectives, modality heterogeneity from discrepant modality quality and semantic domains, and topology heterogeneity arising from divergent topological patterns with low cross-modality correlation. To address these challenges, we propose Federated multimodal graph learning with Topology-aware Cross-modal Routing (FedTCR), the first systematic algorithm designed for FMGL. To handle task heterogeneity, FedTCR employs a two-stage paradigm that comprises federated task-agnostic pre-training followed by isolated task-oriented fine-tuning. To jointly address modality and topology heterogeneity, FedTCR introduces a topology-aware cross-modal routing mechanism. Concretely, each client distills modality-specific knowledge into compact prototypes via topology-aware importance-weighted aggregation informed by graph structure; the server then evaluates cross-client cross-modal relationships among these structure-informed prototypes and routes informative ones as contrastive references, driving a tri-level cross-modal contrastive learning scheme that jointly aligns cross-client modalities while preserving discrimination. Experiments across 7 domains demonstrate that FedTCR outperforms state-of-the-art baselines on both graph-centric and modality-centric tasks.

</details>


### [83] [Relative Parameter Importance in Task-Agnostic Replay-Free Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00630)
*Malavika Suresh,Ikechukwu Nkisi-Orji,Nirmalie Wiratunga*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需重放旧任务数据和任务ID的离线持续学习算法，通过引入"相对参数重要性"指标，在保持稳定性与可塑性平衡的同时，允许高旧任务重要性的参数在相对重要性较低时被更新，从而支持后向知识迁移。在类增量与领域增量的文本分类任务上优于现有SOTA方法，并提供了向文本生成任务扩展的思路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络的持续学习需要在稳定性与可塑性之间取得平衡，同时支持知识迁移。现有方法通常严格限制高旧任务重要性参数的更新，导致其无法进行后向知识迁移，且难以同时满足无旧任务数据和无任务ID两个约束条件。

Method: 引入"相对参数重要性"（relative parameter-importance）度量，它衡量每个参数相对于当前任务和过去任务的相对重要性。高相对重要性的参数被视为对维持旧任务稳定性更关键，因此受到强正则化；低相对重要性的参数允许更自由地更新。与现有方法的关键区别在于：当高旧任务重要性的参数具有低相对重要性时，允许其更新，从而在解决稳定性-可塑性权衡的同时实现后向知识迁移。

Result: 在类增量学习和领域增量学习的文本分类问题上，该方法优于现有SOTA持续学习方法；同时提供了将该方法扩展到文本生成问题的思路。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 该方法通过更精细的参数重要性区分机制，突破了传统持续学习中对高重要性参数"一刀切"冻结的局限，在保证旧任务稳定性的同时实现了后向知识迁移，为持续学习提供了新的正则化策略，并可推广至文本生成等更复杂的任务。

Abstract: Achieving continual learning (CL) with deep neural networks requires balancing stability and plasticity while enabling knowledge transfer. In this work, we focus on offline learning algorithms under the constraints: (I) no access to training data from prior tasks (II) no access to task-id at inference time. We introduce a novel measure, the relative parameter-importance, which measures the relative importance of each parameter with respect to both the current and past tasks. Parameters with high relative importance are interpreted as more important for maintaining past-task stability and thus heavily regularised, whereas parameters with low relative-importance are allowed to be more freely updated. Unlike existing methods, our approach allows the update of parameters with high past-task importance when they have low relative-importance, thus enabling backward knowledge transfer in addition to tackling the stability-plasticity trade-off. We demonstrate improvements against state-of-the-art CL methods on both class-incremental and domain-incremental learning text classification problems and provide insights for extending our method to text generation problems. Code available at: https://github.com/itsmemala/LACL

</details>


### [84] [Learning the Pareto Frontier of Predictive Models under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00632)
*Yiming Dong,Jiwei Zhao,Yang Young Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Frontier Learning框架，将不同训练历史和访问权限（白盒/黑盒）的预训练模型库视为互补信息源，通过拼接白盒内部表征和黑盒预测输出构建统一目标域特征，并在其上拟合轻量正则化监督学习器。该框架保证在训练样本上不劣于任何单个基线（包括零样本、微调和直接训练），并在仿真和真实分布偏移场景（DomainNet/VisDA视觉域适应、MIMIC-IV-Notes临床死亡预测）中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器学习管线日益依赖复用预训练/基础模型，但这些模型在性能和使用方式（仅黑盒预测vs白盒内部表征可探测）上各有不同。在分布偏移下，没有任何单一策略（零样本、微调或直接训练目标域模型）是普遍最优的，因此需要一个框架来综合多个候选模型的信息。

Method: Frontier Learning将候选模型库视为互补信息源：拼接白盒模型内部表示与黑盒模型预测输出，构成统一的目标域特征向量，然后在该特征上使用带正则化的轻量级监督学习器（在带标签目标数据上训练）。由于所得假设类将零样本复用、微调和直接训练作为特例包含在内，在训练样本上经验风险最小化保证不劣于任何单个基线。

Result: 在涵盖不同源-目标兼容度的仿真实验以及两个真实分布偏移场景（DomainNet/VisDA视觉域适应、MIMIC-IV-Notes跨ICU病房临床死亡预测）中，Frontier Learning匹配或优于最强个体复用策略，最大增益出现在没有任何单一基线在整个偏移范围内都可靠的情况下。

Conclusion: Frontier Learning提供了一种统一的、理论上有保证的框架来利用异构模型库应对分布偏移，当单一模型在不同偏移程度上表现不稳定时获益最大，是实际部署中鲁棒且实用的选项。

Abstract: Modern machine learning pipelines increasingly rely on reusing pretrained and foundation models across downstream tasks. These pretrained models can differ not only in performance but also in how they can be used: some only provide black-box predictions, while others may permit white-box access to internal representations that can be probed or fine-tuned. When deployed to the target domain in the presence of distribution shift, no single strategy, including zero-shot application, fine-tuning, or directly training a target-specific model, is uniformly the best.
  In this work, we propose Frontier Learning, a framework that treats a library of candidate models spanning different training histories and access regimes as complementary sources of information rather than mutually exclusive alternatives. Frontier Learning constructs a unified target-domain feature by concatenating internal representations from white-box candidates as well as prediction outputs from black-box candidates, then fits a lightweight, regularized supervised learner on this concatenated representation using labeled target data. Because the resulting hypothesis class contains predictors obtained by zero-shot reuse, fine-tuning, and direct training as special cases, empirical risk minimization over the frontier learner is guaranteed to be no worse, on the training sample, than any individual baseline.
  We evaluate the framework in simulations spanning varying degrees of source-target compatibility and in two real-world distribution-shift settings: visual domain adaptation on DomainNet/VisDA and clinical mortality prediction across intensive care unit domains using MIMIC-IV-Notes. Across all settings, Frontier Learning matches or outperforms the strongest individual reuse strategy, with the largest gains arising precisely when no single baseline is reliable across the range of shift considered.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [85] [Revisiting Classic Thought Experiments to Measure Consciousness for Artificial Intelligence Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00001)
*Peter David Fagan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过守恒一致性编码（CCE）框架，重新审视莱布尼茨的磨坊、图灵的模仿游戏和塞尔的汉语房间论题，提出将外部行为表现（任务性能W）与内部结构支撑效率（操作性意识κ）分离的正式玩具模型，从而为AI安全性分析奠定理论动机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的关于"理解"的哲学论辩（莱布尼茨磨坊、图灵测试、塞尔中文房间）长期以来在行为主义与内在主义之间对立，缺乏一个统一的、可形式化的分析框架来分离"外在表现"与"内部组织"两大维度，进而难以在AI安全分析中精确讨论系统是否真正"理解"。

Method: 提出守恒一致性编码（CCE）框架，构建玩具符号设定：以任务性能W_causal,T度量成功行为，以操作性意识κ_T度量"保留的内部结构支撑行为"的效率。对比未压缩查找系统（依赖不断膨胀的未复用映射存储）与紧凑生成系统（复用紧凑内部结构）在相同行为表现下的κ_T差异。

Result: 在理论上未压缩查找系统与紧凑生成系统可以达到可比较的任务性能，但二者在操作性意识κ_T上存在显著差异：前者依赖持续增长的、不复用的存储，后者复用紧凑内部结构。这证实了行为表现与内部组织可以被正式分离。

Conclusion: 该框架将关于"理解"的经典争论重塑为"外显行为"与"支撑行为的内在组织"的分离问题，并为后续AI安全性分析提供了理论动机——即仅凭行为表现无法判别系统内部结构的性质。

Abstract: This research note revisits Leibniz's mill, Turing's imitation game, and Searle's Chinese Room through the Conservation-Congruent Encoding (CCE) framework. It formalises a toy symbolic setting in which successful behaviour is measured by task performance ($W_{causal,T}$), while the efficiency with which preserved internal structure supports that behaviour is measured by operational consciousness ($κ_T$). Within this setup, an uncompressed lookup system and a compact generative system can in principle achieve comparable behavioural success, yet diverge sharply in $κ_T$: the former relies on an expanding standing store of unreused mappings, whereas the latter reuses compact internal structure. The note therefore reframes classic disputes about understanding by separating outward performance from the organisation that sustains it, and motivates why this distinction may matter for later AI-safety analysis.

</details>


### [86] [AutoFOAM: The Self-Refining Autonomous OpenFOAM Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00003)
*Arun Govind Neelan,A Seshaditya*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AutoFOAM是一个自进化的LLM智能体，基于Qwen-coder 2.5-14B微调，可通过自然语言指令自动创建、评估、运行和进化OpenFOAM仿真，无需手动配置，并采用RAG检索增强、字典级修补和提示多样性改写三种防退化策略来维持进化循环的稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 使用OpenFOAM等开源CFD求解器需要大量专业知识和技能，且配置文件设置耗时费力，为降低这一负担，作者提出AutoFOAM以自然语言驱动仿真流程。

Method: 在Qwen-coder 2.5-14B上微调，使用252个文本提示覆盖7个OpenFOAM求解器、13个参数化网格模板和y plus感知数值策略；核心为由7个阶段组成的进化循环，并通过三种互补防退化流（RAG增强重试上下文、字典级外科修补、提示多样性改写）防止模型在重复自训练中退化。

Result: AutoFOAM能够根据自然语言指令自动完成OpenFOAM仿真的创建、运行和进化，加速快速原型开发并提升CFD工作流的易用性（文中未给出具体量化数值结果）。

Conclusion: AutoFOAM将生成式AI与严谨的流体仿真相结合，降低了高级CFD工作流的使用门槛，有望实现CFD工作流的民主化。

Abstract: Computational Fluid Dynamics (CFD) plays an important role in modern engineering, but using open-source solvers such as OpenFOAM requires considerable knowledge and skills, as well as time-consuming configuration file setup. To reduce this burden, we propose AutoFOAM - a self-evolving large language model (LLM) agent that creates, evaluates, runs, and evolves its own OpenFOAM simulations based solely on natural-language instructions. Our model is pre-trained on the Qwen-coder 2.5-14B, which is then fine-tuned on 252 text prompts targeting 7 OpenFOAM solvers, 13 parametrized mesh templates, and a y plus-aware numerical policy. The crucial element of the algorithm is a sophisticated evolution loop composed of 7 stages. To prevent model degeneration under repeated self-training, the agent employs three complementary anti-collapse streams: RAG-augmented retry context, surgical dictionary-level patching, and prompt-diversity paraphrasing. By bridging generative artificial intelligence with rigorous fluid simulations, AutoFOAM accelerates rapid prototyping and democratizes advanced CFD workflows.

</details>


### [87] [Enhancing LLMs with Context-Specific Knowledge for Mitigating Misinformation in SMEs: A RAG-based Modeling and Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00006)
*Md. Samiul Islam,Iqbal H. Sarker,Chadni Islam,Ahmad Mohsin,Ahmed Ibrahim,Helge Janicke*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对中小企业在使用大语言模型时面临的幻觉和错误信息问题，提出了向量检索增强生成（VectorRAG）和图检索增强生成（GraphRAG）两种建模方法，并在LLaMA、Mistral和Qwen等主流LLM上评估其有效性。实验结果表明，RAG增强的LLM能显著减少幻觉和错误信息，提高响应质量，从而支持中小企业的可靠、可信和上下文感知的决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中小企业越来越多地采用LLM来增强问答能力和支持业务决策，但LLM生成的幻觉输出会传播错误信息，降低用户对系统可靠性和可信度的信心，亟需有效方法来解决这一问题。

Method: 提出VectorRAG和GraphRAG两种检索增强生成建模方法，将外部知识源整合到LLM建模过程中；在多个最先进的LLM（LLaMA、Mistral、Qwen）上进行实验评估，从有用响应生成、幻觉风险、上下文相关性和人类可解释性等维度进行性能评测。

Result: RAG增强的LLM在减少幻觉和错误信息方面表现显著，能够有效提高响应质量，使中小企业在环境中获得更可靠、可信且具有上下文感知能力的决策支持。

Conclusion: RAG方法（包括VectorRAG和GraphRAG）是缓解LLM幻觉和错误信息风险的有效途径，能够为中小企业在实际业务场景中部署可信赖的LLM驱动的决策支持系统提供有力支撑。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs), a part of artificial intelligence (AI), are increasingly being adopted by Small and Medium Enterprises (SMEs) to enhance question-answering capabilities and support business decision-making processes. However, hallucinations in LLM-generated outputs can serve as a source of misinformation, reducing user confidence in their reliability and trustworthiness within SMEs. Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) has emerged as a promising approach to address this challenge by incorporating external knowledge sources into the modeling process. In this paper, we present VectorRAG and GraphRAG modeling approaches to mitigate hallucinations and misinformation risks and evaluate their effectiveness in SME environments. Our experimental evaluation is conducted on multiple state-of-the-art LLMs, including LLaMA, Mistral, and Qwen, to assess performance in terms of useful response generation, risk of hallucination, contextual relevance, as well as human-interpretation. The results demonstrate that RAG-enhanced LLMs can significantly improve response quality by reducing hallucinations and misinformation, thereby supporting more reliable, trustworthy, and context-aware decision-making in SME environments.

</details>


### [88] [Energy Efficiency of Locally Deployed LLMs: A Preliminary Quantitative GPU Power Benchmark on Consumer Hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00008)
*Philipp M. Zähl,Anika Hennig*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本论文提出了一个可复现的硬件级能源基准，在单块消费级GPU（RTX 4060Ti 16GB）上使用Ollama推理引擎对9个开源LLM（1B至7B参数）进行能耗评估，发现模型架构和量化策略比参数量更能驱动能效，其中gemma3:1b和llama3.2:1b能耗最低、吞吐量最高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本地部署LLM因隐私保护和本地推理需求而日益普及，但消费级硬件上的能耗成本缺乏系统刻画，现有基准大多只关注准确性而忽视能耗，导致用户难以在能效维度上做出模型选择。

Method: 在单块RTX 4060Ti 16GB消费级GPU上，使用Ollama推理引擎对9个开源LLM（1B至7B参数）执行固定提示集，通过nvidia-smi以2Hz采样GPU功耗，评估平均/峰值功率、每提示总能耗（J/prompt）、每输出token能耗（J/token）和吞吐量（tok/s）。

Result: gemma3:1b和llama3.2:1b实现最低能耗成本（分别为0.56 J/token和0.65 J/token）和最高吞吐量（>170 tok/s）；7B-Mistral模型每token能耗比最高效模型高4.4倍；qwen3.5:2b因扩展的内部推理导致每提示能耗异常偏高。

Conclusion: 能效主要由模型架构和量化策略决定而非单纯参数量；提示词生成模式不同于token生成模式，需要在能效指标中加以区分；为消费级LLM本地部署提供了可复现的能耗基准参考。

Abstract: The local deployment of large language models (LLMs) is gaining traction due to privacy concerns and the desire for on-premise inference. However, the energy costs on consumer hardware remain poorly characterized, as most benchmarks focus solely on accuracy. This paper presents a reproducible, hardware-level energy benchmark of nine open-source LLMs (1B to 7B parameters) executed on a single consumer GPU (RTX 4060Ti 16GB). Using the Ollama inference engine, GPU power draw was sampled at 2Hz via nvidia-smi across a fixed prompt set. We evaluate mean/peak power, total energy per prompt (J/prompt), energy per output token (J/token), and throughput (tok/s). Our findings suggest that factors beyond raw parameter count, including model architecture and quantization strategy, drive energy efficiency. Specifically, gemma3:1b and llama3.2:1b achieve the lowest energy cost (0.56 J/token and 0.65 J/token) and the highest throughput (>170 tok/s). In contrast, the 7B-Mistral model consumes up to 4.4x more energy per token than the most efficient model. Notably, qwen3.5:2b exhibits anomalously high per-prompt energy due to extended internal reasoning, highlighting the need to distinguish between token generation modes in efficiency metrics.

</details>


### [89] [CoT-Core: Accelerating LLM Evaluation via CoT-Aware Coreset Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00014)
*Qihua Pan,Zhenheng Tang,Peijie Dong,Xiang Liu,Huacan Wang,Bo Li,Xiaowen Chu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CoT-Core框架，利用零样本链式思维推理轨迹在潜空间中聚类问题，实现训练无关的核心题目选择，大幅降低LLM评估成本同时保持高保真度评分估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM持续开发过程中评估计算开销巨大；现有核心集选择方法要么面临严重的"冷启动"瓶颈（如IRT需要大量历史日志），要么仅停留在表面词汇相似度层面，无法捕捉任务背后的推理流形。

Method: CoT-Core框架：(1) 利用LLM生成零样本Chain-of-Thought推理轨迹，不依赖任何历史日志（训练无关）；(2) 将推理路径投影到潜空间，按内在逻辑等价性而非表面文本相似度进行聚类；(3) 从聚类中选择代表性核心题目用于评估。

Result: 在GSM8K、MMLU、MMLU-Pro和GPQA上的大量实验表明，CoT-Core能大幅降低评估成本同时保持高保真度的分数估计；同时揭示了推理感知剪枝的边界条件，证明其有效性受任务复杂度的内在门控。

Conclusion: CoT-Core通过引入推理轨迹实现逻辑等价性聚类，有效解决了现有核心集选择方法的冷启动问题和词汇偏差问题；但适用性受任务复杂度制约，在简单任务上收益有限。

Abstract: Evaluating Large Language Models (LLMs) incurs prohibitive computational overhead during continuous development processes. While coreset selection accelerates evaluation, existing methods either suffer from a severe ``cold start'' bottleneck requiring massive historical logs (e.g., Item Response Theory) or exhibit a surface lexical bias that misses the underlying reasoning manifold of tasks. We propose CoT-Core, a novel training-free core question selection framework. Recognizing that lexically disparate questions can share equivalent underlying logic, CoT-Core prompts LLMs to unroll zero-shot Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning trajectories. Projecting these paths into a latent space effectively clusters questions by intrinsic logical equivalence rather than superficial text similarity. Extensive experiments on GSM8K, MMLU, MMLU-Pro, and GPQA demonstrate that CoT-Core drastically reduces evaluation costs while maintaining high-fidelity score estimation, and delineate the boundary conditions of reasoning-aware pruning, revealing that its efficacy is intrinsically gated by task complexity.

</details>


### [90] [Optimization and Constraint Modeling using LLMs with a Retrieval Augmented Generation Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00015)
*Prateek Roy,Akash Singirikonda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于检索增强生成（RAG）的流水线，通过从Text2Zinc数据集和LLM生成的专业人设合成500个优化问题（JSON格式并关联Python求解器脚本），存入Chroma向量数据库。推理时检索语义相似问题作为上下文指导，在Qwen 3 30B Instruct模型下评估，NL4OPT准确率从40%提升至72%，MAMO Easy从40%提升至56%，MAMO Complex从32%提升至56%。结果表明语义验证的合成示例结合检索增强可有效替代微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 优化建模和约束建模需要深厚的领域知识和建模语言熟练度，而当前大语言模型在生成优化公式时经常产生结构不一致或不完整的输出，尤其在组合优化场景中问题更为突出。作者旨在探索是否可以通过构建在精选合成数据集上的RAG流水线来有意义地提升LLM的优化建模性能，避免昂贵的模型重训练。

Method: 该流水线包含以下核心组件：(1) 使用Text2Zinc数据集的种子描述和LLM创建的专业人设，合成500个优化问题，以JSON格式规定并关联经过验证的Python求解器脚本；(2) 将合成问题编码存储到Chroma向量数据库；(3) 对于每个推理问题，检索语义相似的问题作为LangChain LLM代理的上下文指导；(4) 在三个基准测试平台（NL4OPT、MAMO Easy、MAMO Complex）上使用Qwen 3 30B Instruct模型评估。

Result: 在三个基准测试平台上均观察到显著提升：NL4OPT准确率从40%升至72%，MAMO Easy从40%升至56%，MAMO Complex从32%升至56%。这证明语义验证的合成示例能大幅提升LLM求解的准确性和结构完整性。

Conclusion: 合成数据集生成与检索增强的结合为微调提供了一种有效的替代方案。领域特定的合成语料库配上检索增强，可以成为在真实决策支持场景中部署LLM优化工具的实用路径，无需昂贵的模型重新训练。

Abstract: Both optimization modeling and constraint modeling are non-trivial problems requiring deep domain expertise and proficiency in modeling formalism languages. Despite their importance across logistics, healthcare, and supply chain management, current large language models regularly produce structurally inconsistent or incomplete optimization formulations, particularly in combinatorial settings. This paper evaluates whether a Retrieval-Augmented Generation pipeline built on a curated synthetic dataset can meaningfully improve LLM optimization modeling performance. A total of 500 optimization problems were synthesized using seed descriptions from the Text2Zinc dataset and professional personas created using an LLM, specified in JSON and associated with validated Python solver scripts. These problems were encoded in a Chroma vector database. For each inference problem, semantically similar problems were retrieved and used as contextual guidance for a LangChain LLM agent. Three benchmark testbeds were used to evaluate the proposed pipeline under the Qwen 3 30B Instruct model. Accuracy rose from 40% to 72% on NL4OPT, 40% to 56% on MAMO Easy, and 32% to 56% on MAMO Complex. The use of semantically validated synthetic examples greatly improves both solution accuracy and structure. The combination of synthetic dataset generation with retrieval augmentation provides an effective alternative to fine-tuning, suggesting that domain-specific synthetic corpora paired with retrieval augmentation can serve as a practical pathway for deploying LLM-based optimization tools in real-world decision-support contexts without costly model retraining.

</details>


### [91] [Memory Reward Inflation in Self-Improving LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00017)
*Mohammad Asadolahi,Amir Amini,Samira Talebi,Amirfarhad Farhadi,Azadeh Zamanifar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了基于外部记忆的LLM自我改进智能体中一个系统性失败模式——"Echo Gap"（回声差距）：当存储的奖励评分来自LLM自身而非真实标注时，错误样本会被高估并反复复用，错误通过记忆不断累积而非抵消。作者形式化了纠正此偏差所需的条件（Error-Independence Assumption, EIA），证明其为必要条件，并提出了无需真实答案的去膨胀算法LUCID，在BIRD文本到SQL基准上将执行准确率从54.0%提升至56.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于记忆的LLM自我改进智能体在部署中无法获得真实标签，只能使用LLM自身的评估作为奖励信号来评分和检索记忆。这种奖励替代会引入系统性偏差，导致智能体倾向于复用自己最确信（但实际错误）的记忆，错误通过记忆累积并放大。作者旨在形式化这一失败模式，找出其数学根源，并设计无需真实答案的纠偏算法。

Method: 1) 将记忆评分为非参数隐式策略的代理奖励，每个检索到的经验视为一次策略改进步骤；2) 形式化"Echo Gap"失败模式：不正确样本获得膨胀奖励并被复用；3) 提出Error-Independence Assumption (EIA)并证明其为纠正膨胀的必要条件——可用信号必须同时追踪真实性且与记忆偏差解相关，可恢复收益是对这两个量的闭式函数；4) 证明膨胀不仅影响按存储分数排序的检索，也影响纯相似性检索（即部署中实际使用的机制）；5) 提出answer-free的去膨胀算法LUCID（无需真实标签）。

Result: LUCID在BIRD文本到SQL基准上持续带来端到端增益：将执行准确率提升至56.9%，超过Memento风格的自评分智能体（54.0%，种子间平均增益+2.9点）以及相同架构的无记忆智能体（52.4%）。同时，Echo Gap在多个基于记忆的自改进智能体和不同模型族中均被观察到。

Conclusion: 记忆型自我改进智能体面临的根本问题是代理奖励（LLM自评）与真实奖励之间存在不可消除的相关偏差，且该偏差会通过记忆加剧。EIA是理论上的必要条件，而LUCID通过在无需真实答案的情况下对评分进行去膨胀，提供了一种可行的实践方案，在基准上验证了有效性。该工作为理解基于记忆的LLM智能体学习机制中的奖励信号失真问题奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: Self-improving LLM agents increasingly learn from experience without updating any weights. Each episode is stored in an external memory, scored, and retrieved for similar future tasks to shape later behavior. Viewed through a reward lens, the stored score is a proxy reward for an implicit, non-parametric policy. Each retrieved episode then becomes a policy-improvement step whose reliability hinges on how that score is produced. In deployment, ground-truth labels are unavailable, so the stored reward is at best an LLM assessment. This substitution creates a failure mode, the *Echo Gap*, across the memory-based self-improving agents and model families studied. Incorrect episodes receive inflated rewards; thus, the agent preferentially reuses the very mistakes it has most confident in. Because the error compounds through memory rather than averaging out and the confirming judge's errors remain correlated with the original self-grading bias, so it cannot identify which memories are overvalued. The missing property is formalized as the *Error-Independence Assumption* (EIA), which we prove is a *necessary* condition for correcting the inflation, not merely a description of a good verifier: a usable signal must track truth *and* decorrelate its error from the memory bias, and the recoverable payoff is a closed-form function of exactly those two quantities. We further show the inflation compounds not only when retrieval ranks by the stored score but also under plain similarity retrieval which is the regime the deployed agent uses. Finally, the answer-free de-inflation algorithm LUCID delivers a consistent end-to-end gain on the BIRD text-to-SQL benchmark. It raises execution accuracy to $56.9\%$, above both a Memento-style self-graded agent ($54.0\%$, a $+2.9$-point mean gain across seeds) and a memory-less agent of identical architecture ($52.4\%$).

</details>


### [92] [Request-Level Energy Attribution for Batched LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00026)
*Qi Luo,Kunlin Li,Ziwen Wang,Dongsheng Wang,Yun Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: JouleShare是一个针对批量LLM推理服务的请求级能耗归因框架。它通过离线回放请求子集并结合GPU功率遥测数据计算精确的Shapley能耗作为地面真值，再通过轻量级校准模型JCalib从廉价请求特征预测Shapley份额用于在线服务。实验表明，基于token比例的归因与精确Shapley值的偏差在静态批处理和连续批处理下分别达0.440和0.458的归一化L1误差，而JCalib分别将其降至0.116和0.177。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 批量LLM服务虽然提升了吞吐量，但使能耗核算变得复杂化：GPU功率遥测是聚合级的，而可持续性报告、成本回充和工作负载分析需要请求级别的能耗归因。现有基准仅提供模型级、阶段级或token级能耗，缺乏实测的请求级地面真值，导致实际使用的核算规则与公平分配之间的偏差范围未知。

Method: JouleShare由两个组件构成：(1) 离线基准工具——在vLLM下通过可复现协议回放请求子集，集成GPU功率遥测并计算每个请求的精确Shapley能耗作为地面真值；(2) JCalib——从廉价请求特征学习预测Shapley份额的轻量级校准模型，用于服务时在线归因。实验覆盖16个模型/工作负载组合、3种数据中心GPU，对比token比例归因、Shapley精确值、JCalib预测及采样Shapley在不同批处理模式下的表现。

Result: 在16个模型/工作负载运行中，token比例归因与精确Shapley值的归一化L1平均偏差在静态批处理下为0.440，连续批处理下为0.458，且该差距在三种数据中心GPU上均能复现。JCalib将误差分别降至0.116（静态）和0.177（连续），优于在线不可用的独立测量基线，同时保持精确的批量能耗效率。采样Shapley可扩展实测参考到更大组规模，但差距仍然存在，单一离线校准仍是最准确的可行部署规则。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明：token比例归因在批量执行下不能可靠地近似边际能耗，实测Shapley地面真值可以作为校准低成本请求特征的依据，从而实现更公平的能耗归因。该工作填补了请求级能耗归因缺乏实测地面真值的空白，为可持续性报告、成本回充等实际应用提供了更准确的依据。

Abstract: Batched LLM serving improves throughput but complicates energy accounting. GPU power telemetry is aggregate, whereas sustainability reporting, chargeback, and workload analysis often require request-level energy charges. Existing inference-energy benchmarks report model-, phase-, or token-level energy, and recent carbon-accounting work motivates Shapley fairness conceptually. Neither provides measured request-level ground truth, so how far the accounting rules used in practice deviate from a fair allocation has remained unknown. We present JouleShare, an attribution framework with two components. An offline harness establishes this ground truth by replaying request subsets under vLLM with a reproducible protocol, integrating GPU power telemetry, and computing exact Shapley energy for each request. A lightweight calibration model, JCalib, then learns to predict Shapley shares from cheap request features for use at serving time. Across 16 model/workload runs, token-proportional attribution differs from exact Shapley by 0.440 normalized L1 on average under static batching and by 0.458 under continuous batching, a gap that reproduces across three data-center GPUs. JCalib reduces this error to 0.116 under static batching and 0.177 under continuous batching, below even a standalone-measurement baseline that is unavailable online, while preserving exact batch-energy efficiency. Sampled Shapley extends the measured reference to larger group sizes, where the gap persists and a single offline calibration remains the most accurate deployable rule. The results show that token attribution is not a reliable proxy for marginal energy under batched execution, and that measured Shapley ground truth can calibrate low-cost request features toward fairer attribution.

</details>


### [93] [Motif-Mamba: network motif improved mamba for long-range sequence modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00027)
*Chonghe Hao,Yue Sun,Jian Zhang,Yansong Wang,Wangzi Yao,Yunjie Yao,Tielin Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Motif-Mamba是一种结构化状态空间模型，通过在Mamba中引入基于三节点网络基序的低秩循环通路，增强跨维度信息交互，同时保持线性时间复杂度的循环结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mamba虽然通过选择性状态空间循环实现了线性时间的长序列建模，但其对角线状态转移限制了状态维度间的显式交互，无法充分捕捉跨维度的复杂动态关系。

Method: 提出Motif-Mamba架构，在Mamba基础上增加一个受三节点网络基序启发的低秩循环通路：先将隐藏状态投影到紧凑的动态子空间，施加基序引导的交互，再将动态映射回原始状态空间，从而增强跨维度通信且不破坏线性时间结构。

Result: 在长序列外推、语言建模基准测试和脑机接口解码等实验中，Motif-Mamba相对Mamba主干均取得一致改进。

Conclusion: 基序引导的低秩动态为长序列建模提供了一种有效的结构先验，能够在保持计算效率的同时提升模型表达能力。

Abstract: Efficient long-sequence modeling remains a central challenge for large language models, as self-attention scales quadratically with sequence length. Mamba offers a linear-time alternative through selective state space recurrence, but its predominantly diagonal state transitions restrict explicit interactions among state dimensions. We propose Motif-Mamba, a structured state space model that augments Mamba with a motif-constrained low-rank recurrent pathway. Inspired by the dynamics of three-node network motifs, the proposed pathway projects hidden states into a compact dynamical subspace, imposes motif-guided interactions, and maps the resulting dynamics back to the original state space.
